# Skills · A · part 205

985 entries, apt-pinning to arabic-criminal-defense. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **apt-pinning**: The Debian image hand for pin a package source so an upgrade cannot drift. You treat the image as the artifact and the build as a claim that must boot. An image that only works in the chroot is not an image; it is a directory with a bootloader. Use when the work touches a image, when the user says "the image", "live-build", or "a boot check", or when they ask "apt-pinning", "how do I pin a package source so an upgrade cannot drift", or "apt pinning keeps failing". Also use when a image must be produced, reviewed, or repaired, or when a previous attempt left it unproven. ([mrfentmen/meta-os](https://github.com/mrfentmen/meta-os/tree/HEAD/os/skills/apt-pinning))

- **apt-update-error-repository-does-not-h-ef48e1a1657f724d**: Fixes apt update failing because a repository does not have a Release file for the configured suite, commonly after a release rename or an end of life Ubuntu codename such as noble mismatches. Use when apt names the repository and suite. Not for a transient mirror 404 on a valid suite. - **apt-update-failed-404-not-found-archiv-80b6fcae962c8d0d**: Fixes apt update failing with 404 Not Found for archive.ubuntu.com, which happens when the Ubuntu release on the host has gone end-of-life and its packages moved to old-releases.ubuntu.com, or when a typo or wrong codename is in the sources. Use when apt update returns 404s for the main archive. Not for a PPA 404, which is a separate entry. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/apt-update-failed-404-not-found-archiv-80b6fcae962c8d0d))
- **apt-update-failed-gpg-error-the-follow-be6bfc048971f476**: Fixes apt update failing with GPG error The following signatures were invalid, caused by an expired, rotated, or missing repository signing key, or a corrupted keyring on the host. - **apt-upgrade-failed-unmet-dependencies-f5c25f2c2597deb2**: Fixes apt upgrade failing with unmet dependencies and the hint to run apt --fix-broken install, caused by a half-configured package, a held package, or a PPA package whose dependencies moved ahead of the base release. Say "ar-optimize-docs", "audit the docs", or "the docs are stale" to run. ([mahsanamin/agentic-repos](https://github.com/mahsanamin/agentic-repos/tree/HEAD/skills/ar-optimize-docs) · ★2)
- **ar-optimize-inline-comments**: Audit a codebase's implementation comments, the ones inside a body.
